Automatic Tax Category IDs and Names


This document provides an overview of the tax categories available in MxP (MxP). These categories help ensure accurate tax calculations across products, services, and subscriptions.


Overview

In MxP, taxes can be applied to transactions, subscriptions, and products using Tax Category IDs and Tax Category Names. On the UI, businesses can select the Tax Category Name, which corresponds to a unique Tax Category ID on the backend used for calculations and reporting. Proper assignment ensures accurate tax handling and compliance with local and international tax regulations.


How to Use Tax Categories

  1. Assigning Categories: When creating or editing a product or default for a sub-account, select the appropriate Tax Category Name from the available options on the UI.

  2. Calculating Taxes: MxP uses the associated Tax Category Name to apply the correct tax rate based on the customer’s location and your configured nexus addresses.

  3. For APIs, use the Category IDs and Category Names local to MxP.


Notes

  • Regularly review your tax categories to stay compliant with updates to local tax laws.

  • If a tax category does not apply to a transaction, select "Non-Taxable" or the relevant Tax Category Name to ensure no tax is applied.
